## 4. Inherited-obligation intake

The compact inherited tables preserve the full ledger-template row contract through these defaults unless a row states otherwise: no enforced executable artifact is claimed for this documentation-only Draft; the verification ID/state cell names every current or allocated method; the oracle owner/status is the author/ledger closer for objective Draft checks and the named later owner for a non-current allocation; the evidence/notes cell binds the exact source and candidate subject; and no compact row creates authority, implementation, or a pass.

### 4.1 JAN-CSAA-000@0.3.0 rows

Each row preserves one exact stable source row from the closed 000 refresh ledger. VERIFICATION-001 and successor VERIFICATION-002 passed 001's applicable downstream carriage and audited every noncurrent allocation without performing the allocated future activity.

| `CSAA-000-REQ-040` | `JAN-CSAA-000@0.3.0` / §4.2, L256 | SHALL — 'apps/rph-demo/tsconfig.json' extends the generated '.svelte-kit/tsconfig.json'. The present generated configuration identifies the app include surface, but its freshness was not established because the write-producing 'svelte-kit sync' operation was not authorized. Compiler-confirmed app support therefore SHALL retain the generated configuration's revision-bound provenance, digest, and freshness basis; until that evidence exists, the app project context SHALL be reported as configured-but-unverified or partial rather than compiler-confirmed. ### 4.3 Intake exclusions and concern routing

| Source surface not expanded into 001-owned rows | Rationale |
| --- | --- |
| `JAN-CSAA-000` REQ-001–039 | Charter identity, sponsor package, and whole-corpus capability declarations remain parent authority. 001's detailed allocation begins at REQ-180; architecture-relevant subject rules are carried from REQ-040. |
| `JAN-CSAA-000` REQ-206–641 | Detailed obligations belong to 002–011 or later whole-corpus phases. Main §17.2 records 001's exact boundary to each owner without importing its concern. |
| `JAN-CSAA-000` REQ-686–735, REQ-751–753, REQ-755–782 | Whole-corpus completion, implementation readiness/DoD, W0 mechanics, and other-member content are not 001 performance. Applicable no-existence/provider-neutral controls are carried as REQ-736–750, REQ-754, and REQ-783. |
| Canon professional-work object/lifecycle clauses not listed in §4.2 | 001 does not govern PWA, PWU, Undertaking, professional-objective, assurance-disposition, or Baseline semantics. Only architecture, authority, evidence, agent-boundary, persistence-safety, oracle, and drafting obligations engaged by 001 are ledgered. |

## 5. States, row rules, conflicts, and gaps

### 5.1 Allowed obligation dispositions

- `APPLICABLE_NOW`
- `ALLOCATED_TO_ACTIVE_DOCUMENTATION_SUBPHASE`
- `ALLOCATED_TO_POST_LEDGER_SELF_REVIEW`
- `ALLOCATED_TO_LATER_EXECUTION`
- `ALLOCATED_TO_LATER_WAVE`
- `ALLOCATED_TO_LATER_LIFECYCLE`
- `CONDITION_SATISFIED_SUCCESSOR_MODEL_CONTROLS`
- `DEFERRED_BY_CITED_AUTHORITY`
- `WAIVED_BY_CITED_AUTHORITY`
- `DIVERGENT_WITH_FINDING`
- `NOT_APPLICABLE_WITH_RATIONALE`

### 5.2 Allowed implementation states

- `NOT_STARTED`
- `PLANNED`
- `IMPLEMENTED`
- `NOT_REQUIRED_CURRENT_PHASE`

### 5.3 Allowed verification states

- `NOT_RUN`
- `PASSED`
- `FAILED`
- `BLOCKED`
- `INCONCLUSIVE`
- `STALE`
- `UNSUPPORTED`
- `NOT_REQUIRED_CURRENT_PHASE`

Implementation and verification are independent. All 483 applicable-now documentation rows are `IMPLEMENTED / PASSED` under direct-current VERIFICATION-003; all 76 post-ledger-self-review, later-lifecycle, later-execution, superseded, deferred, successor-controlled, or not-applicable rows remain `NOT_REQUIRED_CURRENT_PHASE`. An allocation identifies an owner and gate but creates no artifact, authority, performance, or executable result. Objective closure does not perform cross-package reconciliation, author self-review, final refresh, independent assurance, or implementation.

### 5.4 Requirement-row rules

Each row retains stable ID, concern owner, exact source/version/section, non-weakened obligation, applicability, disposition authority, substantive site, implementation state, verification binding, oracle owner where applicable, and exact-subject evidence. Independent obligations are not compressed. Deferral, waiver, divergence, allocation, and not-applicability remain distinct from implementation and verification.

### 5.5 Apparent conflicts and gaps

| Record ID | Surface | Conflict or missing information | Safe default | Evidence | Blocking scope | State |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `JAN-CSAA-001-LEDGER-GAP-001` | Inherited intake and corrected local catalog | The exact `0.3.1` correction preserves all 558 predecessor rows, adds singular `FLW-025`, and received direct-current exhaustive objective verification under VERIFICATION-003 | Preserve exact row order, obligations, allocations, new-row singularity, and nonpasses; rerun affected methods after any later source or candidate change | VERIFICATION-003; exact Draft/OPEN identities; [JAN-CSAA-W4-RESIDUAL-001-003-005-CORRECTIVE-RECONCILIATION-001@0.1.0](<JAN-CSAA-W4 - Residual 001 003 005 Corrective Reconciliation and Synchronized Ledger State Record.md>) §§5–9 records only administrative reconciliation and review activation; preliminary findings and final implementation refresh remain open | None for corrective objective closure or residual reconciliation; final refresh and corrective SELF remain open | `CORRECTIVE_SUCCESSOR_OBJECTIVELY_VERIFIED — RESIDUAL_001_003_005_RECONCILIATION_RECORDED` |
